Sandhi patterns
  • The refrain’s नमोऽस्तु (नमः + अस्तु: -aḥ → -o, then pūrvarūpa) closes every verse from 11.8 to 11.26.
  • Visarga sandhi throughout: -aḥ → -o before voiced sounds (नो देवि, 11.24, 11.27; खड्गो भवतु, 11.28; ततो मां, 11.45), -āḥ → -ā (सुरा वह्नि-, 11.2; सकला जगत्सु, 11.6; देवा ऊचुः, 11.38), visarga → s/ś before t/c (भयेभ्यस्त्राहि, 11.24; ततस्तौ, 11.42; भूयश्च, 11.46) and → r after other vowels (तुष्टुवुरिष्टलाभाद्, 11.2; मातर्जगतः, 11.3; रूपैरनेकैर्बहुधा, 11.30; भीतेर्भद्रकालि, 11.26).
  • Vowel sandhi of every kind: savarṇa-dīrgha (स्थितासि, 11.4; वरदाहं, 11.37; शाकम्भरीति, 11.49), guṇa (विश्वस्योपरतौ, 11.9; प्रसीदेशे, 11.23; यन्मनसेच्छथ, 11.37), vṛddhi (त्वयैकया, 11.6; चैन्द्रि, 11.19; तत्रैव, 11.49), yaṇ (देव्युवाच, 11.36, 11.40; विवेकदीपेष्वाद्येषु across the printed line, 11.31; करिष्याम्यरिसंक्षयम्, 11.55) and pūrvarūpa (महाशक्तिधरेऽनघे, 11.15; ममत्वगर्तेऽतिमहान्धकारे, 11.31; वैवस्वतेऽन्तरे, 11.41).
  • Consonant sandhi: t → n before a nasal (विपन्नराणाम्, 11.29; यन्मुनीन्, 11.47; तन्मे, 11.50, 11.52), final n → ṃś/ṃs before c/t (उत्पातपाकजनितांश्च, 11.34; वैप्रचित्तांस्तु, 11.43), t → d before a voiced sound (इष्टलाभाद् विकाशि-, 11.2).
  • One declared hiatus: प्राप्ते | अष्टाविंशतिमे at the pāda break of 11.41; the vowels also stand apart at the half-verse break of 11.34 (नयाशु | उत्पात-).
  • The Gita Press marks avagrahas where no letter is lost or where the vowel is long (महाऽविद्ये, 11.22; पापेभ्योऽनः, 11.27; बहुधाऽऽत्ममूर्तिम्, 11.30; कृत्वाऽसंख्येय-, 11.53); the padaccheda follows the grammar.
Samāsa constructions
  • Upapada tatpuruṣas of the agent make most of the hymn’s epithets: -हरा/-हारिणी (प्रपन्नार्तिहरे, 11.3; वृत्रप्राणहरे, 11.19; विश्वार्तिहारिणि, 11.35), -दा/-प्रदायिनी (स्वर्गापवर्गदे, 11.8; परिणामप्रदायिनि, 11.9; वरदा, 11.35, 11.37), -धरा/-धारिणी (ब्रह्माणीरूपधारिणि, 11.13; त्रिशूलचन्द्राहिधरे, 11.14), and the names शाकम्भरी (with the inserted m, 11.49) and विन्ध्याचलनिवासिनी (11.42).
  • Bahuvrīhis picture the Mothers by weapon, mount and face (शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गगृहीतपरमायुधे, 11.16; दंष्ट्रोद्धृतवसुंधरे, 11.17; दंष्ट्राकरालवदने, शिरोमालाविभूषणे, 11.21) and give the future names (रक्तदन्तिका, 11.45; शताक्षी, 11.47; असंख्येयषट्पदम्, 11.53).
  • Long nested compounds, two of them filling a whole printed line (शरणागतदीनार्तपरित्राणपरायणे, 11.12; विकाशिवक्त्राब्जविकाशिताशाः, 11.2), and असुरासृग्वसापङ्कचर्चितः (11.28).
  • Karmadhārayas with महा- (महासुर, 11.2, 11.30, 11.41; महाविद्या, महारात्रि, महाऽविद्या, 11.22), rūpakas (वक्त्राब्ज, 11.2; ममत्वगर्त, 11.31) and karmadhārayas with भूत ‘being’ (आधारभूता, 11.4; सर्वभूता, 11.7; शक्तिभूते, 11.11).
  • Dvandvas (चराचर, samāhāra, 11.3; स्वर्गापवर्ग, 11.8; सृष्टिस्थितिविनाशानाम्, 11.11; मयूरकुक्कुट, 11.15) and negative compounds (अनघे, 11.15; अनावृष्ट्याम्, अनम्भसि, अयोनिजा, 11.46).
Grammatical observations
  • पुष्टि (11.22) and भूति (11.23) are stem-form vocatives for पुष्टे and भूते, kept as printed; the printed पुष्टिस्वधे is divided as two vocatives.
  • शक्ते (11.9) can belong to शक्ति ‘power’ or शक्ता ‘able’; it is read as शक्ति, as in 11.5 and 11.11. नियते (11.23) is read as Niyati, with नियता ‘the self-restrained’ as the alternative.
  • स्तव्यपरा परोक्तिः (11.6) is read as a definition of praise in apposition to स्तुतिः; the reading as epithets of the Goddess is flagged.
  • In 11.27 the printed पापेभ्योऽनः is read as पापेभ्यः + नः, and the second नः as genitive with पापेभ्यः (‘from our sins’).
  • -रूपेण/-स्वरूपेण in 11.14, 11.18 and 11.20 are instrumentals of form standing among vocatives, not vocatives.
  • यत् … तम् (11.37) has a neuter relative and a masculine correlative, read as a generic ‘whatever (boon)’.
  • Purāṇic forms in the prophecy: the ordinal अष्टाविंशतिमे (11.41), the parasmaipada future निरीक्षिष्यामि of an ātmanepadin root (11.47) and the future वधिष्यामि (11.49, 11.53).
  • The printed आवृष्टेः (11.48) is divided as आ + वृष्टेः, ‘until the rain’.
Philosophical themes
  • Nārāyaṇī and the one Goddess of many forms
  • Refuge and protection
  • Binding and liberating power
  • The boon and the promise of descent
  • The future forms and their names
Open questions
  • 11.10: सर्वमङ्गलमङ्गल्ये (Gita Press, kept) / सर्वमङ्गलमाङ्गल्ये (GRETIL, Freund) — Textual Verification Required; the kept form is the one generally recited, and the metre and sense are the same.
  • 11.22: श्रद्धे पुष्टिस्वधे ध्रुवे (Gita Press) / श्रद्धे पुष्टे स्वधे ध्रुवे (GRETIL; Freund पुष्टि स्वधे) — a reading; पुष्टिस्वधे is divided as two vocatives.
  • 11.22: महाऽविद्ये (Gita Press, kept) / महाविद्ये (GRETIL, Freund) — ‘great nescience’ or ‘great knowledge’; both readings are traditional.
  • 11.27: पापेभ्योऽनः (Gita Press, also in its appendix) / पापेभ्यो नः (GRETIL, Freund) — orthographic; the words and the metre are the same. The missing space the entry’s flag notes (देविपापेभ्यो) is resolved by the typographical correction in corrections.json, which restores देवि पापेभ्योऽनः.
  • 11.2: the Gita Press विकाशि-/विकाशित- (with ś) is often printed विकासि-/विकासित- (with s) elsewhere; no variant is recorded in corrections.json, the Gita Press reading is kept, and either root gives the same image.
  • Interpretive doubts, none about the reading itself, are flagged on 11.6 (स्तव्यपरा परोक्तिः), 11.13 (the derivation of क्षरिका), 11.23 (भूति as a vocative; नियते; बाभ्रवी), 11.31 (the construction of the locatives), 11.37 (यत् … तम्), 11.39 (सर्वाबाधा), 11.47 (यत् as ‘since’ or ‘when’) and 11.48 (आवृष्टेः); 11.10, 11.22 and 11.27 carry the variants above.
Status
Complete with Noted Variants
